64 object VariableHelper {
65   /**
66    * creates a variable.  The expr is set with 'set var' allowing queries, the default is null.
67    *
68    * NB declare or replace is used.
69    *
70    * @param stableName
71    * @param ddl
72    */
73   def createVar(stableName: String, ddl: String, expr: String): Unit = {
74     // Defaults can't have subqueries
75     val defaultCommand = s"declare or replace variable `$stableName` $ddl default null;"
76     SparkSession.active.sql(defaultCommand)
77     val setCommand = s"set var `$stableName` = $expr;"
78     SparkSession.active.sql(setCommand)
79   }
80 }
81 
82 object Encoders extends EncodersImplicits {
83 
84   def rowTypedEnc(rowType: DataType): TypedEncoder[Row] =
85     new TypedEncoder[Row]()(ClassTag(classOf[Row])) {
86       override def nullable: Boolean = agnosticEncoder.nullable
87 
88       override val agnosticEncoder: AgnosticEncoder[Row] = ShimUtils.rowEncoder(rowType.asInstanceOf[StructType])
89     }
90 
91 }
